Existing methods heavily rely on auxiliary handcrafted spatial priors (e.g., masks, poses) for editing control. However, these priors are prone to failure in unconstrained real-world videos and often compress rich visual context into incomplete structural signals. Furthermore, standard reconstruction objectives fail to fully capture try-on-specific human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose InstructVVT, an instruction-driven and reference-guided video virtual try-on framework based on a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates without inference-time spatial priors. Our core insight is to recover fine-grained control directly from the input triplet (source video, reference garment, and instruction) via a dual-level reference conditioning scheme. Specifically, an MLLM infers semantic edit tokens for target disambiguation and structural preservation, while a lightweight conditioning pathway explicitly injects fine-grained visual garment details. Finally, we design a try-on-specific reward and utilize the DiffusionNFT algorithm to align the model with human preferences. Extensive experiments on ViViD-S and TripVVT-Bench demonstrate that InstructVVT outperforms state-of-the-art open-source methods in garment fidelity, structural preservation, and temporal consistency, despite requiring fewer inference-time controls.

</details>


### [35] [Owner3D: Ownership-Guided Style Writing for Training-Free Localized 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14078)
*Suchang Tao,Kaifeng Shi,Zhiyan Liu,Zhuoyuan Jiang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Owner3D是一个无需训练的3D局部风格化框架，通过所有权引导的风格写入、边界双槽位和表面优先纹理读取，在大型重建模型中实现精确的局部样式注入，显著减少样式泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型重建模型中进行局部3D风格化面临两大挑战：紧致的triplane特征在目标和背景表面间共享导致样式泄漏，以及语义边界附近的表观歧义难以解决。

Method: 1) 所有权引导风格写入：将参考样式限制注入到目标区域，生成单一局部风格化triplane，无需额外训练。2) 边界双槽位：为目标和非目标区域维护独立的局部特征源以解决边界歧义。3) 表面优先纹理读取：分层结合表面、3D和triplane所有权证据，在可见性不完整时稳健恢复外观。

Result: 在Google Scanned Objects和PartNet构建的基准上，Owner3D在目标区域风格保真度和非目标区域外观保持方面持续优于现有3D风格化方法，相比StyleSplat和LAENeRF分别减少86.4%和89.9%的外观泄漏。

Conclusion: Owner3D通过将局部外观控制直接集成到LRM重建流程中，实现了无需训练的高质量局部3D风格化，有效解决了样式泄漏和边界模糊问题。

Abstract: Localized 3D stylization aims to modify the appearance of a specified object part while preserving the remaining surfaces. In large reconstruction models (LRMs), this task is challenging because style is injected into intermediate appearance representations before rendering, while compact triplane features are shared across target and non-target surfaces, causing style leakage and boundary ambiguity. We propose Owner3D, a training-free framework for localized 3D stylization that integrates localized appearance control directly into the LRM reconstruction process. Specifically, Owner3D introduces ownership-guided style writing to restrict reference-style injection to target regions, producing a single localized stylized triplane without additional training while avoiding separate global style and appearance representations. To resolve appearance ambiguity near semantic boundaries, we further introduce boundary dual slots that maintain separate local feature sources for target and non-target regions. Finally, a surface-first texture readout hierarchically combines surface, 3D, and triplane ownership evidence to robustly recover appearance under incomplete visibility. Experiments on a benchmark constructed from Google Scanned Objects and PartNet demonstrate that Owner3D consistently outperforms existing 3D stylization methods in target-region style fidelity and non-target appearance preservation, reducing appearance leakage by 86.4% and 89.9% compared with StyleSplat and LAENeRF, respectively.

</details>


### [36] [CoDS: Robust Collaborative Perception via Expert-driven Detection and BEV Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14085)
*Jinlong Wang,Yuang Jia,Junhong Lin,Nannan Li,Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDS框架，通过协同感知中的联合检测与BEV分割训练，利用Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM)量化特征质量分布，Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE)提取差异化特征，以及Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI)进行任务间双向交互，在OPV2V和V2V4Real数据集上超越现有基线，并保持对多源噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协同感知中，多源噪声（如位姿误差、通信延迟）会降低融合特征质量，从而限制感知性能。现有方法缺乏对特征质量不一致性的显式建模与处理，需要更鲁棒的协同感知框架。

Method: 1) 提出Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM)显式量化融合特征质量分布；2) 基于CoRM设计Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE)模块，针对不同特征质量需求提取差异化特征；3) 提出Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI)，通过检测与BEV分割任务的互信息注入来缓解噪声退化。

Result: 在OPV2V和V2V4Real两个公开数据集上，CoDS在检测和BEV分割两个任务上均超越现有基线方法，并在多源噪声条件下保持稳定的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联合训练检测与BEV分割能为协同感知提供自然的互补约束，结合显式的质量量化与任务间交互机制，可以有效提升协同感知在复杂噪声环境下的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Collaborative perception breaks through single-view limitations via multi-agent information exchange. However, multi-source noise such as pose errors and communication delays degrades fusion feature quality, constraining perception performance. Joint training of detection and BEV segmentation provides a natural remedy, where segmented road regions help constrain target distributions and detection bounding boxes help recover ambiguous segmentation boundaries. To this end, we propose a robust Collaborative perception framework with expert-driven Detection and bev Segmentation (CoDS). To address spatial inconsistency in fusion quality, we first introduce the Collaborative Reliability Map (CoRM) to explicitly quantify feature quality distribution. Based on CoRM, we design the Semantic Mixture-of-Experts (S-MoE) module to extract differentiated features for inconsistent feature demands. Finally, to further mitigate feature noise degradation, the Bidirectional Task Complementary Interaction (BTCI) refines task-aware features through bidirectional injection. Extensive experiments on OPV2V and V2V4Real datasets show that our CoDS surpasses existing baselines on both tasks and maintains stable robustness under multi-source noise. Code: https://github.com/JinlongW128/CoDS and https://openi.pcl.ac.cn/OpenAIDriving/CoDS.

</details>


### [37] [Fixed-Budget Gaussian Volume Encoding with Structure-Aware Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14112)
*Michael R. Martin,Joseph Insley,Victor A. Mateevitsi,Silvio Rizzi,Kwan-Liu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将标量场编码为各向异性高斯基元的方法，在固定预算下进行原位压缩，支持后置可视化状态自由调整而无需重新编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学模拟产生的标量体数据生成速度远超存储、传输和加载能力，而原位缩减只能用有限的模拟资源，需要高效的压缩编码方案。

Method: 将标量场编码为各向异性高斯基元，完整基元集通过局部场结构解析分配（含位置、方向、形状），再直接对标量场进行细化（无密集化、剪枝或计数变化）。预算决定编码存储量，配合迭代调度控制细化时间预算，并采用截断感知的场求值加速。

Result: 截断感知场求值使编码时间最多减少51倍；140万高斯基元在桌面GPU上4分钟内编码十亿体素体积；压缩有效配置在五个数据集上达到15.0-38.7 dB PSNR，压缩比2.2倍到40000倍以上。

Conclusion: 由于基元保留标量属性而非烘焙外观，单个紧凑模型可服务所有后续可视化状态，支持后置传递函数、颜色映射、光照和视点变化，同时预编码结构统计可预测一次性分配在额外容量下收益有限的情况。

Abstract: Scientific simulations often produce scalar volumes faster than they can be stored, transferred, and loaded, while in situ reduction must use only a limited share of simulation resources. This work encodes scalar fields as anisotropic Gaussian primitives under a fixed budget. The complete primitive set is allocated analytically from local field structure, including position, orientation, and shape, then refined directly against the scalar field without densification, pruning, or count changes. The selected budget determines encoded storage before refinement and, together with the iteration schedule, provides a controllable refinement-time budget. In a controlled benchmark, truncation-aware field evaluation reduces encoding time by up to 51x; 1.4 million Gaussians encode a billion-voxel volume in at most four minutes on one desktop GPU, with reduced-iteration refinement completing in under one minute. Across five datasets spanning 2.1 million to 1.1 billion evaluated voxels, compression-useful configurations achieve 15.0-38.7 dB PSNR at compression ratios from 2.2x to over 40,000x. Pre-encoding structure statistics characterize fields for which one-shot allocation yields limited gains from additional capacity. Because primitives retain scalar attributes rather than baked appearance, a single compact model serves every subsequent visualization state - supporting post-hoc transfer-function, colormap, lighting, and viewpoint changes without re-encoding.

</details>


### [38] [HiCo-GS: Hierarchical Context Aggregation and Geometric Consistency for Octree Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14136)
*Wei Zhang,Shengkai Yu,Shiqiang Gong,Qi Zhang,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiCo-GS提出一种基于八叉树锚点的高保真城市级新视角合成框架，通过跨层上下文聚合（CLCA）解决层级特征隔离问题，并结合深度-法向几何一致性正则化（DNGC）抑制漂浮伪影，在多个城市级基准上达到最先进渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于八叉树的锚点高斯泼溅方法存在跨层特征隔离问题：每层锚点特征独立优化、缺乏层级间通信，导致建筑立面色差和纹理区域过平滑。

Method: 提出两个互补模块：(1) CLCA利用八叉树空间包含结构，将每层上下文向量聚合为父-自-子三元组，通过带残差连接的轻量MLP融合，实现双向层级先验注入；(2) DNGC通过alpha加权一致性损失约束渲染法向与深度推导法向的一致性，并配合渐进式边缘感知平滑损失，利用城市几何的平面先验抑制漂浮伪影。同时引入包含8座中国古塔、每座超1200张图像的新数据集China-Pagoda。

Result: 在Mill19、UrbanScene3D、MatrixCity和China-Pagoda四个数据集上的大量实验表明，HiCo-GS在真实与合成城市基准上均达到最先进的渲染质量，且几何重建更干净。

Conclusion: HiCo-GS通过跨层信息流通和几何一致性正则化有效解决了八叉树锚点高斯的层级隔离问题，显著提升了城市级场景重建的保真度与几何质量。

Abstract: Octree-based anchor Gaussian Splatting has emerged as a scalable representation for city-scale novel view synthesis, where multi-level anchors adaptively capture scene content from coarse building structures to fine architectural details. However, we identify a fundamental limitation in existing methods: cross-level feature isolation, where each level's anchor features are optimized independently with no inter-level communication, causing color drift on building facades and over-smoothing in textured regions. We present HiCo-GS, a high-fidelity reconstruction framework with two complementary modules. Cross-Level Context Aggregation (CLCA) enables bidirectional hierarchical prior injection by leveraging the octree's spatial containment structure to aggregate per-level context vectors into parent-self-child triplets, fused via a lightweight MLP with residual connection. Coarse-level structural priors flow down to inform fine-level anchors, while fine-level detail statistics feed back to prevent over-smoothing, at negligible computational overhead. Depth-Normal Geometric Consistency (DNGC) regularization enforces agreement between rendered normals and depth-derived normals through an alpha-weighted consistency loss, complemented by edge-aware smoothness losses with progressive warmup that exploit the strong planar priors ubiquitous in urban geometry to suppress floating artifacts. We further introduce the China-Pagoda dataset comprising 8 ancient Chinese pagodas with over 1,200 images each, featuring dense ornamental carvings, curved multi-layer eaves, and repetitive fine-grained textures. Extensive experiments on Mill19, UrbanScene3D, MatrixCity, and China-Pagoda demonstrate that HiCo-GS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality and substantially cleaner geometry across real-world and synthetic urban benchmarks.Code: https://github.com/WZ-CS/HiCo-GS.

</details>


### [39] [SPARGen: Unifying Spatial Perception and Reasoning through Native Multimodal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14138)
*Jinsheng Quan,Jianhua Li,Siyi Xie,Xuanke Shi,Kewang Deng,Zukai Chen,Feifei Shao,Lei Yang,Quan Wang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPARGen是一个统一的多模态框架，将3D重建、密集对应和空间推理三种空间任务统一为指令条件生成任务，通过紧凑结构化输出与密集几何场生成的结合，在单一原生多模态生成模型中实现跨任务的共享表征学习，并在多个基准上取得具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常使用任务特定的架构或外部几何模块分别处理3D重建、对应关系建立和空间推理，导致同一物理场景的互补表示之间缺乏知识迁移，限制了模型对空间感知和推理能力的整体提升。

Method: SPARGen将3D重建、密集对应和空间推理统一为指令条件生成任务；将紧凑的结构化与语言输出序列化为token序列，同时以图像对齐形式生成密集几何场，使空间监督信号能够共同塑造原生多模态生成模型中的共享表示。

Result: 在3D重建、对应关系和空间推理的多个基准测试中，SPARGen在单一原生多模态生成框架内实现了跨异构空间任务的具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: SPARGen验证了将多种空间任务统一为生成任务并在单一多模态模型中共享表示的有效性，表明无需任务特定模块即可实现多种空间能力的协同学习。

Abstract: Spatial perception and reasoning from visual observations require recovering geometric structure, establishing correspondences, and understanding spatial relations. Existing approaches typically address these capabilities separately using task-specific architectures or external geometric modules, limiting knowledge transfer among complementary representations of the same physical scene. We introduce SPARGen, a unified multimodal framework that casts 3D reconstruction, dense correspondence, and spatial reasoning as instruction-conditioned generation tasks. SPARGen serializes compact structured and linguistic outputs as token sequences while generating dense geometric fields in image-aligned forms, enabling spatial supervision to jointly shape shared representations within a native multimodal generative model. Experiments across benchmarks for 3D reconstruction, correspondence, and spatial reasoning show that SPARGen achieves competitive performance across heterogeneous spatial tasks within a single native multimodal generative framework.

</details>


### [40] [PISA: A Pseudo-Individual Source-Domain Feature Adaptation Framework for Test-Time Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14142)
*Ziyan He,Xiongtai Yang,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PISA是一种无需源数据的开放词汇目标检测测试时自适应方法，通过腐败不变特征提取器、特征对齐模块和多尺度对齐框架，将CLIP视觉特征转化为密集的伪个体源域特征用于监督，在COCO-C等基准上超越现有方法3.92%的AP@50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源无关的OVOD-TTA方法在初始预测较差时精度显著下降（依赖重打分或伪标签自训练），而传统源域估计方法只能捕获稀疏抽象的分类特征，缺乏检测所需的密集具体特征，导致域偏移下性能退化。

Method: 提出PISA框架，包含三个核心组件：1)腐败不变特征提取器(CIFE)利用CLIP视觉特征在损坏图像上的不变性；2)特征对齐模块(FAM)负责预训练；3)多尺度对齐框架(BAA)用于适配阶段，将腐败不变特征转化为接近原始源域的伪个体源域特征。

Result: 在VOC-C、COCO-C和LVIS-C三个损坏基准及三个基础模型上的实验表明，PISA显著提升了原始模型的定位精度和类别识别准确率，在COCO-C上AP@50%超越现有方法3.92%，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: PISA通过用密集具体的伪个体源域特征替代不可靠的伪标签信号进行监督，有效解决了开放词汇目标检测在域偏移下的性能退化问题，且无需访问源域数据即可实现最优性能。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection test-time adaptation (OVOD-TTA) aims to address the performance degradation that pre-trained base models suffer when encountering image-domain shifts. Existing source-free OVOD-TTA methods rely either on refined test-time information for re-scoring or on pseudo-labels for self-training, leading to significant accuracy degradation when initial predictions are poor. Meanwhile, most conventional source-domain estimation methods recover abstract, sparse representations suitable for the classification task, but fail to capture the dense, concrete features required for detection. To address these issues, we propose PISA, a novel source-free OVOD-TTA method that can be seamlessly integrated into open-vocabulary visual backbones. The core components of our method are the Corruption-Invariant Feature Extractor (CIFE), the Feature Alignment Module (FAM), and a multi-scale alignment framework (BAA). To capture detection-suitable features, we develop CIFE to exploit the invariance of CLIP's visual features across corrupted images, ensuring robustness against various corruptions. We further develop FAM and BAA for the pre-training and adaptation to transform the corruption-invariant features into pseudo-individual source-domain features that are close to the original source-domain features. In this way, dense and concrete pseudo-individual source-domain features are used for supervision instead of unreliable pseudo-label signals. Experiments on the corrupted VOC-C, COCO-C, and LVIS-C benchmarks across three base models demonstrate that PISA substantially improves both the localization precision and the category recognition accuracy of the original models. Notably, PISA achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring access to source-domain data, surpassing existing methods by 3.92% in AP@50% on COCO-C.

</details>


### [41] [Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14144)
*Yijiang Li,Yijun Liang,Yunjie Tian,Bingyang Wang,Ke Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Di Fu,Philip Torr,Nuno Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出自监督视觉在线策略蒸馏（S²VOPD），通过从学生端减去信息而非向教师端添加特权信息来构建不对称性，利用增强视图实现无需任何特权监督的在线策略蒸馏，在六个细粒度感知基准上显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉在线策略蒸馏依赖教师-学生之间的信息不对称，而这种不对称通常来自更大的教师模型或特权监督（如参考答案、真实标注区域）。作者提出一个根本性问题：当没有特权信息可用时，不对称性从何而来？答案是反转不对称性的来源——不向教师添加信息，而是从学生端减去信息。

Method: S²VOPD构建在线策略学习信号：将教师分布（基于原始图像）蒸馏到学生分布（基于同一图像的强增强视图）。通过系统性探索视觉增强的设计空间，发现三个关键原则：不对称性至关重要（四种增强族都提升性能，而对称自蒸馏会降低性能）、增强强度适中时性能最佳、增强与任务之间必须保持一致性（完全移除问题相关证据的增强会产生大但不提供信息的差异）。

Result: 在六个细粒度感知基准上，S²VOPD将Qwen3.5-4B从70.7%提升至77.4%，超过所有对比的开源模型（包括高达235B的Qwen3-VL），并超越GPT-5.4。在保持训练数据不变的情况下，恢复了有特权信息方法所实现改进的96%。

Conclusion: 本文证明了一种不依赖任何特权信息构建有效在线策略蒸馏不对称性的新范式，通过简单的增强不对称就能获得接近特权蒸馏方法的收益，为视觉在线策略蒸馏提供了更广泛适用且无需额外监督的解决方案。

Abstract: Visual on-policy distillation relies heavily on an informative teacher-student asymmetry, through either a larger, stronger teacher or privileged supervision, such as reference answers or ground-truth regions of interest. This raises a fundamental question: where can informative asymmetry come from when nothing privileged is available? We answer this by inverting where the asymmetry comes from. Rather than adding privileged information to the teacher, we subtract information from the student. This asymmetry creates the same effective learning signal for free as a teacher with access to information unavailable to the student, without ground-truth annotations, rewards, or a separate stronger teacher model. Building on this principle, we introduce Self-Supervised Visual On-Policy Distillation (S$^2$VOPD), a simple yet effective method that constructs on-policy learning signals from asymmetric augmented views. S$^2$VOPD distills the teacher's distribution conditioned on the original image on-policy into the student distribution conditioned on a strongly augmented view of the same image. We systematically explore a broad design space of visual augmentations and uncover that (1) asymmetry matters: all four augmentation families improve performance, while symmetric self-distillation degrades it; (2) strength matters: performance peaks at a moderate strength; and (3) the gap must remain task-consistent: augmentations that completely remove the question-relevant evidence can induce large but uninformative discrepancies. Across six fine-grained perception benchmarks, S$^2$VOPD improves Qwen3.5-4B from 70.7% to 77.4%, above all open-source models compared, up to Qwen3-VL at 235B, and surpasses GPT-5.4. While holding training data the same, it recovers 96% of the improvement achieved by methods with privileged information. Website is at https://williamium3000.github.io/s2vopd

</details>


### [42] [CSG-Mamba: A Convolutional Scoring Gating Vision State Space Network for Endoscopic Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14146)
*Yuliang Wang,Jiaqi Wu,Jiaye Song,Shuxia Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSG-Mamba，一种基于卷积评分门控的视觉状态空间模型，用于内窥镜息肉分割。该模型在VM-UNet风格的U形编码器-解码器瓶颈处插入卷积评分门控模块，通过逐点卷积和大核深度卷积生成局部空间评分图，以乘法门控方式重新校准状态空间特征，在Kvasir-SEG和CVC-ColonDB数据集上取得了优于基准的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内窥镜图像中的息肉分割面临低对比度边界、黏膜纹理干扰、镜面高光和设备差异等挑战。尽管视觉状态空间模型（SSM）具备高效的长距离建模能力，但现有Vision Mamba分割模型将2D特征转换为1D扫描序列，可能削弱局部几何连续性并对不规则轮廓过度平滑。

Method: 基于VM-UNet风格的非对称U形编码器-解码器架构，在语义丰富的瓶颈处插入卷积评分门控（CSG）模块。CSG通过逐点卷积和大核深度卷积生成局部空间评分图，并通过乘法门控重新校准状态空间特征。

Result: 使用三个随机种子进行实验，CSG-Mamba在Kvasir-SEG上达到0.9220 Dice和15.87 HD95，在CVC-ColonDB上达到0.7418 Dice和0.6570 mIoU，在大多数重叠度和召回率指标上优于基准模型，同时保持有竞争力的边界精度。

Conclusion: 卷积评分门控机制能够有效恢复Vision Mamba在1D序列化过程中丢失的局部几何连续性，从而提升息肉分割的准确性，且不牺牲边界精度。

Abstract: Accurate polyp segmentation is critical for computer-aided colonoscopy, yet endoscopic images often contain low-contrast boundaries, mucosal texture interference, specular highlights, and device-dependent appearance shifts. Vision State Space Models (SSMs) provide efficient long-range modeling with linear complexity, but existing Vision Mamba segmentation models typically convert 2D features into 1D scanning sequences, which may weaken local geometric continuity and over-smooth irregular contours. We propose CSG-Mamba, a convolutional scoring gating Vision State Space network for endoscopic polyp segmentation. Built on a VM-UNet-style asymmetric U-shaped encoder-decoder, CSG-Mamba inserts a Convolutional Scoring Gating (CSG) module at the semantically rich bottleneck. CSG generates a local spatial score map through pointwise and large-kernel depthwise convolutions and recalibrates state-space features by multiplicative gating. Experiments with three random seeds show that CSG-Mamba achieves 0.9220 Dice and 15.87 HD95 on Kvasir-SEG, and 0.7418 Dice and 0.6570 mIoU on CVC-ColonDB, outperforming the baselines on most overlap and recall metrics while maintaining competitive boundary accuracy.

</details>


### [43] [SCVIB: Editable State-Conditioned Visual Instance Binding forMulti-Turn Personalized Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14148)
*Xiongtai Yang,Ziyan He,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出可编辑状态条件下的视觉实例绑定（SCVIB），一个多轮定位新设置，并引入TT-VG方法，通过目标状态转移树和视觉证据接地适配，将定位准确率从60.13%提升至70.27% Joint@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在解析最终引用时并不能保证有效利用对应的视觉证据进行查询端定位，存在引用解析与视觉证据使用之间的脱节问题，需要建立受控测试平台并解决多轮个性化定位中的实际挑战。

Method: 提出TT-VG方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 目标状态转移树（TSTT）——将可见交互编译为协议定义的事件，在版本化目标状态上执行并解析最终查询引用到对应支持证据；(2) 视觉证据接地适配（VEGA）——在轨迹派生的同实例对上训练，利用视觉证据包对解析后的实例进行支持条件接地。

Result: TT-VG达到70.27% Joint@0.5；在匹配目标解析条件下，VEGA超过最强对比方法16.20个点。在Counter-Recency和Rollback场景中提升最大，这些场景需要路由到非最新或恢复的支持证据。

Conclusion: SCVIB可作为多轮个性化定位的受控测试平台，有效利用解析后的支持证据进行查询端同实例定位是多轮个性化定位的核心挑战。

Abstract: We introduce editable state-conditioned visual instance binding, a multi-turn localization setting in which several support-defined instances are introduced across turns and protocol-defined state events determine the final target. We instantiate this setting as SCVIB, comprising 1,050 manually verified support--query base pairs and 1,500 episodes spanning five visual domains, three difficulty levels, and four target-state dependency groups. Direct Seq-free inference reaches only 60.13\% Joint@0.5, indicating that resolving the final reference does not ensure effective use of the corresponding visual evidence for query-side localization. We address this gap with TT-VG (Transition-Tree Visual Grounding), which combines a Target-State Transition Tree (TSTT) with Visual Evidence Grounding Adaptation (VEGA). TSTT compiles the visible interaction into protocol-defined events, executes them over versioned target states, and resolves the final-query reference to the corresponding support evidence. Adapted on trajectory-derived same-instance pairs, VEGA performs support-conditioned grounding of the resolved instance using a Visual Evidence Package. TT-VG reaches 70.27\% Joint@0.5; under matched target resolution, VEGA exceeds the strongest comparison method by 16.20 points. Gains over direct inference are largest on Counter-Recency and Rollback, which require routing to non-latest or restored support evidence. Together, these results establish SCVIB as a controlled testbed and highlight the effective use of resolved support evidence for query-side same-instance localization as a central challenge in multi-turn personalized localization.

</details>


### [44] [Concept Guidance: Precise, Training-Free Latent Control for Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14172)
*Nikolai Röhrich,Isabell Hans,Felix Krause,Björn Ommer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Concept Guidance (CoG) 方法，利用概念级互信息发现扩散模型中特定结构生成定位于特定网络层，通过强化概念相关层的影响来实现无需额外训练的精确目标引导，在多个模型上均取得性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型存在两大缺陷：(1) 缺乏连续、概念特定引导的固有机制（如精确控制图像美观程度）；(2) 在需要高局部一致性的任务（如生成文字或手部）中可靠性不足。作者希望解决这些问题。

Method: (1) 提出概念级互信息（concept-wise mutual information）的新概念，发现不同层之间存在与概念相关的大差异，表明特定结构的生成定位于网络不同部分；(2) 基于此洞察提出 Concept Guidance (CoG)，量化每层概念特定影响，然后通过加权组合"跳过概念相关层"所生成的预测来引导去噪过程。该方法无需额外训练、外部模型、梯度或提示工程。

Result: 在多种目标和流行模型（PixArt-alpha、SD3、SD3.5、FLUX.1-dev）上均展现出性能提升，验证了方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: CoG 是一种精确、目标特定的引导方法，可在现成模型中直接使用，显著提升了扩散模型在概念特定引导和高局部一致性任务上的表现。代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have two major drawbacks that severely limit their practical utility: (1) standard models lack an intrinsic mechanism for continuous, concept-specific guidance (e.g., for precisely controlling how aesthetically pleasing an image looks), and (2) they lack reliability for tasks requiring high local coherence (e.g., generating text or human hands). To tackle these issues, we introduce a novel notion of concept-wise mutual information and find large, concept-dependent differences between individual layers, demonstrating that the generation of specific structures is localized in distinct parts of the network. We exploit this insight by reinforcing the impact of concept-relevant layers in Concept Guidance (CoG), a precise, target-specific guidance method that works for models out-of-the-box without additional training, external models, gradients, or prompt engineering. CoG first quantifies each layer's concept-specific impact and then guides denoising using a weighted combination of predictions generated with concept-relevant layers skipped. We demonstrate performance increases across various targets and popular models like PixArt-alpha, SD3, SD3.5, and FLUX.1-dev. Code is available at https://github.com/CompVis/concept_guidance

</details>


### [45] [LightTeaNet: A Weakly Supervised Lightweight CNN for Multi-Label Tea Leaf Disease Detection and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14178)
*Naif Haider Chowdhury,Md Rahim,Syed Farhan Hasan,Murad Hasan,Prithwiraj Bhattacharjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LightTeaNet，一种轻量级卷积神经网络，用于茶叶病害的弱监督多标签分类和病斑定位。该模型仅依赖图像级标签，通过类激活映射（CAM）实现病害区域自动定位，避免了对大量边界框标注的依赖。模型在精度（0.9615）、召回率（0.8772）和F1分数（0.9179）上表现出色，mAP@0.50达到0.1810，验证了其在资源受限条件下进行智能农业病害监测的有效性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统茶叶病害检测依赖人工检查，存在不一致、劳动密集和监控成本高等问题；而常规目标检测模型（如YOLO）需要大量边界框标注，标注成本高昂。因此需要一种无需精细标注、轻量且高效的深度学习方案来替代传统方法。

Method: LightTeaNet采用弱监督学习策略，仅需图像级标签进行多标签分类训练；利用类激活映射（CAM）自动定位病害区域；引入深度可分离卷积（Depthwise Separable Convolutions）提升参数效率；结合通道注意力机制（Channel Attention）增强特征判别能力。

Result: LightTeaNet在茶叶病害数据集上取得精度0.9615、召回率0.8772、F1分数0.9179，定位性能mAP@0.50为0.1810，且无需任何人工标注即可实现具有竞争力的定位效果。

Conclusion: LightTeaNet验证了弱监督+轻量级CNN架构在农业病害监测中的可行性，提供了一种可解释且资源高效的智能检测框架，在标注成本和模型性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Tea is known as an important crop in many parts of South and Southeast Asia, yet the production of tea is still hampered by the multiple diseases that decrease the quantity and quality. Traditional methods of inspection, which are manual, are not consistent, labor-intensive, and depend on extensive monitoring. This paper introduces a lightweight convolutional neural network (CNN) designed for weakly supervised multi-label classification and disease localization in tea leaves called LightTeaNet. LightTeaNet learns directly from image-level labels and employs Class Activation Mapping (CAM) to localize disease-affected regions automatically, unlike conventional object detection models such as YOLO, which require extensive bounding box annotations. For Parameter efficiency, the network integrates Depthwise Separable Convolutions, and for enhanced feature discrimination, it integrates Channel Attention. LightTeaNet has achieved a Precision of 0.9615, a Recall of 0.8772, and an F1-score of 0.9179, while it shows mAP@0.50=0.1810 without any manual annotations, which delivers a competitive localization performance in the experimental results. These results validate the model as an interpretable as well as a resource-efficient framework for intelligent disease monitoring in agriculture.

</details>


### [46] [RankT2I: A Submodular Framework for Discovering Interpretable and Diverse Semantics in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14226)
*Ritika Allada,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了RankT2I，一种无需训练、模型无关的框架，能够自动发现文本到图像（T2I）模型在给定图像上可编辑的语义。该方法利用多模态视觉语言模型收集候选语义，并通过子模目标函数将语义发现建模为集合选择问题，从而筛选出相关、可编辑且多样化的语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2I模型在图像编辑方面取得了显著进展，但识别模型能够成功编辑的语义仍然困难。传统方法需要用户手动指定要修改的语义，耗时且需要大量试错。因此，需要一种自动化方法来发现可编辑的语义。

Method: RankT2I采用两步方法：(1) 利用多模态视觉语言模型在给定视觉域中收集广泛的候选语义集；(2) 将语义发现问题建模为集合选择问题，使用子模目标函数来识别既相关、可编辑又多样化的语义子集。该方法无需训练且与具体模型无关，适用于扩散模型和FLUX模型。

Result: 实验表明，RankT2I在多个领域的文本到图像编辑模型中能够高效地帮助用户发现广泛的编辑语义，并且在性能上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RankT2I提供了一种自动化的语义发现方案，减轻了用户在T2I图像编辑中手动指定语义的负担，并展现了良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) models have revolutionized the field of image generation and editing. However, identifying semantics that a T2I model can successfully edit in an image continues to be a challenging task. Most existing approaches require users to manually specify semantics to modify a particular image, a time-consuming process that often involves extensive trial and error. In this paper, we present RankT2I, a novel, training-free, and model-agnostic framework that automates the discovery of editable semantics in diffusion and FLUX-based models. Given a visual domain, we first utilize a multimodal vision-language model to gather a broad set of candidate semantics. We then frame semantic discovery as a set selection problem and use a submodular objective to identify semantics that are relevant, editable, and diverse. Our method helps users efficiently identify a wide range of semantics for text-to-image editing models across several domains while outperforming existing methods.

</details>


### [47] [AppleScab-LT: A Longitudinal Real-Field Apple Scab Dataset for Temporal Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14235)
*Aamir Hilal,Shabir Ahmad Sofi,Neeraj Goel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了AppleScab-LT，一个用于苹果黑星病纵向监测的真实田间数据集，包含21条纵向叶片序列、2,101张高分辨率图像和264个渐进时间样本，通过系统化果园监测、专家验证和全面的质量保证框架构建，为时序病害分析和精准农业提供了可靠资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有植物病害数据集多为单时间点的静态图像，缺乏反映田间自然条件下病害随时间和严重程度演变的纵向数据，限制了对病害时序演化的分析能力。

Method: 采用研究问题驱动的框架，通过系统化果园监测收集数据，包括纵向叶片追踪、专家引导的病害验证、多边形标注、叶片隔离、病害严重度量化和时间序列构建；并应用标准化标注协议、专家验证、自动化完整性检查、序列级验证和时间一致性分析等质量保证措施；基于像素严重度、颜色强度严重度和归一化相对严重度量化病害描述符。

Result: 构建了包含21条纵向叶片序列、2,101张高分辨率图像和264个渐进时间样本的AppleScab-LT数据集，捕获了严重度累积、进展速率、监测时间和叶片间进展的变异性，并提供标准化的定量病害描述符。

Conclusion: AppleScab-LT为时序病害智能分析、病害进展建模、精准农业和未来作物健康监测提供了可靠的数据资源。

Abstract: The development of reliable plant disease monitoring systems is constrained by limited longitudinal datasets capturing disease progression under natural field conditions. Although existing plant disease datasets have advanced image-based recognition, most consist of static images acquired at a single time point, limiting analysis of temporal disease evolution and severity progression. To address this gap, this study presents AppleScab-LT, a longitudinal real-field dataset developed to monitor apple scab progression through repeated observations of individually tracked infected leaves. Guided by a research-question-driven framework, the dataset was systematically developed, validated, and characterized for reliable longitudinal disease analysis. AppleScab-LT was constructed through systematic orchard monitoring under natural environmental conditions, incorporating longitudinal leaf tracking, expert-guided disease verification, polygon-based annotation, leaf isolation, disease severity quantification, and temporal sequence construction. A comprehensive quality assurance framework, including standardized annotation protocols, expert validation, automated integrity checks, sequence-level verification, and temporal consistency analysis, was applied throughout curation. The dataset contains 21 longitudinal leaf sequences, 2,101 high-resolution images, and 264 progressive temporal samples from repeated monitoring of same infected leaves. It captures variability in severity accumulation, progression rates, monitoring duration, and inter-leaf progression. Quantitative disease descriptors based on pixel severity, color-intensity severity, and normalized relative severity provide standardized measurements for temporal disease analysis. AppleScab-LT provides a reliable resource for temporal disease intelligence, disease progression modelling, precision agriculture, and future crop health monitoring

</details>


### [48] [Zero-Shot Skeleton-Based Action Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14243)
*Hongsong Wang,Pengbo Yan,Yang Zhang,Qiuxia Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了零样本骨架动作预测（ZS-SkAA）这一新任务，要求在仅观察早期骨架序列的情况下识别未见过的动作类别。作者构建了基线模型（时空特征提取器+互信息估计与最大化模块）和基于NTU RGB+D数据集的基准协议，实验证明了该模型作为强基线的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有骨架动作预测方法假设训练时涵盖所有动作类别，但在真实世界中不可避免地会出现新动作，限制了实际部署。因此需要研究零样本条件下的骨架动作预测任务。

Method: 提出一个基线模型，包含两部分：(1) 时空特征提取器用于从早期骨架序列中提取部分视觉特征；(2) 互信息估计与最大化模块，通过估计并最大化部分视觉特征与语义类别嵌入之间的互信息，实现跨模态对齐，从而增强对未见类别的泛化能力。同时构建了基于NTU RGB+D数据集的ZS-SkAA基准评估协议。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D数据集上的实验表明，该基线模型在零样本动作预测任务上取得了较高的准确率，验证了其作为ZS-SkAA任务强基线的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了ZS-SkAA作为面向真实世界系统的重要研究方向，使机器人等系统能够对未见过的动作进行泛化预测。

Abstract: Action anticipation (AA) aims to recognize ongoing human or humanoids actions from partial observations, enabling robots to predict intentions before the actions are completed. Although skeleton-based AA offers efficiency advantages, existing approaches assume that all action classes are seen during training, which limits their deployment in real-world scenarios where novel actions inevitably arise. To address this gap, we study the new task of Zero-Shot Skeleton-Based Action Anticipation (ZS-SkAA). This task requires recognizing unseen action classes using only limited early-stage skeleton sequences, combining the challenges of partial observations, temporal dynamics, and zero-shot generalization. To establish foundational research for ZS-SkAA, we introduce:(1) A baseline model comprising a spatio-temporal feature extractor and a mutual information estimation and maximization module. This baseline model explicitly aligns partial visual features with semantic class embeddings across modalities by estimating and maximizing their mutual information, enhancing generalization to unseen classes.(2) A benchmark protocol using the NTU RGB+D dataset, which is adapted for rigorous ZS-SkAA evaluation. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model as a strong baseline for ZS-SkAA, achieving high zero-shot accuracy on NTU RGB+D. This work establishes ZS-SkAA as a vital research direction for real-world systems requiring generalization to novel actions.

</details>


### [49] [On the Robustness of Temporal Vision-Language Models for Surgical Endoscopy Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14262)
*Darakshan Rashid,Raza Imam,Ufaq Khan,Muhammad Bilal,Shazad Ashraf,Dwarikanath Mahapatra,Mohammad Yaqub,Muhammad Haris Khan,Imran Razzak,Brejesh Lall,Lena Maier-Hein,Yutong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Endo-C6，一个包含六种内窥镜现实扰动（失焦、雾霾、运动模糊、噪声、电灼烟雾和数据包丢失）的紧凑损坏基准，用于评估时间视觉语言模型（TVLM）在手术视频理解中的鲁棒性。作者在294个数据集级评估中基准测试了3个近期手术TVLM基线，并提出了RobustEndoCLIP（通过VeRA进行少样本参数高效调优），在损坏性能上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床上内窥镜视频常受失焦、烟雾、模糊等真实采集伪影影响，导致分布偏移可能破坏视频-文本对齐，但现有TVLM在此类临床真实条件下的鲁棒性尚未被充分表征。

Method: 构建Endo-C6基准，涵盖六种内窥镜现实扰动（固定高严重度），应用于公开的胃肠道内窥镜和腹腔镜胆囊切除术视频；采用标准化提示协议，在均值与最坏情况下基准测试3个手术TVLM基线；提出RobustEndoCLIP，通过VeRA进行少样本参数高效微调。

Result: 现成TVLM在内窥镜特定损坏下表现出严重的最坏情况崩溃；轻量级少样本适配（RobustEndoCLIP）可大幅提升损坏性能与鲁棒性，且不改变基于提示的交互界面。

Conclusion: Endo-C6可作为标准化的鲁棒性评估基准，推动更可靠的临床视觉语言系统发展。

Abstract: Temporal vision-language models (TVLMs) offer a reusable, prompt-based interface for surgical video understanding, yet, their robustness under clinically realistic acquisition artifacts in endoscopy remains insufficiently characterized. In practice, degradations such as defocus, haze, motion blur, noise, cautery smoke, and packet loss introduce structured distribution shifts which may compromise video-text alignment. We study the robustness of temporal VLMs under such shifts caused by corruptions in clip frames. We introduce Endo-C6, a compact corruption benchmark of six endoscopy-realistic perturbations evaluated at a fixed high severity, and apply it to public Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy and laparoscopic cholecystectomy videos. Under a standardized prompt protocol, we benchmark 3 recent surgical TVLM baselines and analyze robustness in both mean and worst-case settings, spanning 294 dataset-level evaluations. Finally, we present RobustEndoCLIP, obtained by few-shot parameter-efficient tuning with VeRA, outperforming existing TVLM baselines. Our findings show that off-the-shelf TVLMs can exhibit severe worst-case collapse under endoscopy-specific corruptions, whereas lightweight few-shot adaptation can substantially improve corrupted performance and robustness without changing the prompt-based interface. We expect Endo-C6 to support standardized robustness reporting and promote more reliable clinical vision-language systems.

</details>

Abstract: Forecasting crop growth across agricultural landscapes is important for improving the productivity, resilience, and operational management of farming systems. In this work, we investigate whether Earth observation time series and meteorological drivers can be used to predict future canopy development at country scale. We focus on winter wheat and formulate crop growth prediction as forecasting future leaf area index (LAI) trajectories beyond the last available Sentinel-2 observation. We evaluate this task on a multi-year dataset which spans the entire country of Switzerland, containing over 20 million pixel-level Sentinel-2-derived LAI time series paired with meteorological variables. Because cloud cover and revisit gaps leave LAI supervision sparse, models fit the few valid (cloud-free) LAI observations yet oscillate implausibly between them, producing trajectories no real canopy could follow. We introduce a lightweight unimodal shape regulariser which improves trajectory plausibility with negligible loss in accuracy. We compare deep learning sequence-to-sequence (Seq2Seq) models with classic machine learning baselines and show that Seq2Seq models generalise well across years, achieving $\mathrm{R}^2$ above 0.8 and consistently outperforming conventional approaches. Together, these results demonstrate that remote sensing and weather-driven sequence modelling can learn crop growth dynamics at landscape scale. S

### [51] [MAGneT-3D: Monocular and Domain-Generalizable Temporal 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14282)
*Mohamed Kotb,Johannes Meier,Christoph Reich,Oussema Dhaouadi,Luis Denninger,Daniel Cremers*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAGneT-3D，首个面向域泛化的单目时序3D目标检测方法。通过域鲁棒锚点生成器（DRAG）在推理时自适应生成3D提议，结合时序精化与身份合并（TRIM）策略，消除对静态可学习查询的依赖，在跨数据集零样本迁移中显著优于基线（NDS从12.1%提升至18.6%），同时保持域内精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的单目3D检测器使用可学习查询，其参数拟合训练数据的空间分布（如视场角），导致跨数据集和跨环境泛化能力严重下降，尤其在单目视频场景中问题更为突出。

Method: 1) DRAG：推理时自适应生成3D提议替代静态可学习查询；2) TRIM：时序精化和身份合并策略，降低对特定3D提议的依赖；3) 建立覆盖nuScenes、Waymo、Lyft和ONCE的跨数据集基准。

Result: 在零样本域转移设置下，MAGneT-3D在所有基线上表现最优，NDS从12.1%提升至18.6%，同时在域内精度上也有提升。

Conclusion: 通过动态生成提议与时序精化策略，有效解决了基于查询检测器的域泛化瓶颈，兼顾了跨域鲁棒性与域内性能，为单目时序3D检测提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Monocular temporal 3D detection aims to detect objects in 3D, given a monocular video. Query-based 3D detectors unify detection and cross-view association, but their learnable queries fit the spatial distribution of the training data (e.g., field-of-view). We show that this issue is especially severe when these models are applied to monocular video, hindering generalization to unseen datasets and environments. To address this limitation, we introduce MAGneT-3D, the first method for domain-generalized monocular temporal 3D object detection. Instead of relying on static learnable queries, we propose a Domain-Robust Anchor Generator (DRAG) approach that adaptively derives 3D proposals during inference. To further enable domain generalization, we propose a Temporal Refinement and Identity Merging (TRIM) strategy, reducing dependence on specific 3D proposals. To enable comprehensive domain-generalization evaluation, we establish a cross-dataset benchmark spanning nuScenes, Waymo, Lyft, and ONCE. Under zero-shot domain shifts, MAGneT-3D outperforms all baselines, improving NDS from 12.1% to 18.6% while also increasing in-domain accuracy.

</details>


### [52] [Seeing Red, Thinking Bad: Color Bias in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14286)
*Kohsuke Ide,Ryousuke Yamada,Yoshihiro Fukuhara,Hirokatsu Kataoka,Yutaka Satoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）如何处理以图像形式呈现的文本，发现视觉样式偏见（如颜色和对比度）会显著影响VLM的语义分析结果，导致模型偏离人类语义理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在工业决策系统（如招聘辅助和推荐系统）中被广泛使用，因此需要深入分析VLM如何处理视觉和文本信息，特别是视觉样式偏见对模型理解的影响。

Method: 提出"隐形视觉提示"（Stealth Visual Prompts），通过微妙改变文本的视觉样式（如颜色和对比度）但保留语义内容，系统性地控制渲染文本中单词的视觉样式，并测量其对VLM分析的影响，同时分析视觉扰动对视觉编码器潜在表征的影响。

Result: 实验发现：将正面词着绿色会持续使情感预测偏向正面方向，导致VLM经常无法正确识别文本中的负面词汇；降低文本与背景的对比度会增加对视觉显著线索的依赖，导致更多错误的VQA输出。该行为与颜色变化引起的视觉编码器潜在表征变化相关。

Conclusion: 渲染文本的视觉样式可以引导VLM的解释，使其偏离人类的语义理解，这表明视觉样式偏见是VLM需要关注的重要问题。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) are increasingly used in industrial decision-making systems, such as recruitment support and recommendation. This motivates careful analysis of how VLMs process visual and textual information. In this work, we study how VLMs interpret text rendered as an image, and investigate the influence of visual styling biases. To this end, we introduce Stealth Visual Prompts, which subtly change visual styling of text, such as color and contrast, while preserving semantic content. Using these prompts, we systematically control the visual styling of words in text and measure their impact on the analysis performed by VLMs. We further analyze how such visual perturbations affect the latent representations of the vision encoder. From our experiments, we observed that coloring positive words in green consistently shifts sentiment predictions toward a positive direction. As a result, VLMs often fail to properly account for negative words present in the text. Our analysis suggests that this behavior is correlated with changes in the latent representations of the vision encoder induced by color variations. In addition, we show that reducing text--background contrast increases reliance on visually salient cues and leads to more incorrect Visual Question Answering (VQA) outputs. These results suggest that the visual styling of rendered text can guide VLMs' interpretation in ways that diverge from human semantic understanding.
  Project page: https://github.com/KohsukeIde/color-bias-vlm

</details>


### [53] [Conditional Neural Optimal Transport for Predicting Cellular Phenotypes from Molecular Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14293)
*Gauthier Avité,Maxime Sanchez-Renauld,Nicolas Bourriez,Auguste Genovesio*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分子条件化神经最优传输（NOT）的模型，用于从分子结构和阴性对照表型预测细胞表型，在未见过的活性分子上优于基线方法，并识别出分子编码器是泛化性能的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高内涵显微镜能够系统地表征细胞对化学扰动的响应，但化学空间规模巨大，穷举式表型表征在实验上不可行。因此需要计算模型来预测图像衍生的表型，而无需实际获取对应处理的细胞。

Method: 将分子诱导的表型预测建模为图像表示空间中的归纳条件传输问题。首先评估经典最优传输基线，然后引入分子条件化神经最优传输（NOT）模型，该模型使用Monge-Gap正则化训练目标，以分子结构为条件信息，学习将阴性对照未扰动表型传输至扰动表型。

Result: NOT模型能够恢复分子特异的表型效应，同时减少显微镜相关的技术变异，便于跨实验批次比较。在未见过的活性分子上，模型优于基线方法，证明化学条件化传输可以泛化到训练中未观察到的分子。压缩表示空间中的传输可提升性能和可扩展性。

Conclusion: NOT为从分子结构和阴性对照表型预测细胞表型提供了有前景的框架，同时指出开发更具信息量的分子表示是提升分布外性能的关键方向。

Abstract: High-content microscopy enables systematic profiling of cellular responses to chemical perturbations, but the scale of the chemical space makes exhaustive phenotypic characterization experimentally infeasible. This motivates computational models that can predict image-derived phenotypes without acquiring the corresponding treated cells. We formulate molecule-induced phenotype prediction as an inductive conditional transport problem in image representation space. Given a negative-control phenotype and the structure of a molecule, we aim to predict the phenotype induced by the corresponding molecule. We first evaluate classical optimal transport baselines and show that static couplings do not yield useful predictions on large-scale phenotypic image datasets. We then introduce a molecule-conditioned Neural Optimal Transport (NOT) model with a Monge-Gap regularization training objective that learns to transport negative-control unperturbed phenotypes toward perturbed phenotypes using molecular structure as conditioning information. NOT recovers molecule-specific phenotypic effects while reducing microscopy-associated technical variation, thereby facilitating comparisons across experimental batches. On unseen active molecules, the model outperforms baseline approaches, demonstrating that chemically conditioned transport can generalize beyond the molecules observed during training. We identified the molecular encoder as the main limitation to this generalization, while transport in a compressed representation space improves performance and scalability. These results establish NOT as a promising framework for predicting cellular phenotypes from molecular structure and negative-control phenotypes, while highlighting the development of more informative molecular representations as a key direction for improving out-of-distribution performance.

</details>


### [54] [Spatial Message Passing in Language Space for Pathology Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14309)
*Jing-Cheng Yang,Hao-Jung Wang,Jinhao Du,Yang Hu,Ming-shan Tsai,Jens Rittscher,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出空间语言消息传递（SLMP）框架，通过将WSI区域表示为空间文本图，在纯语言空间内进行空间推理，无需微调MLLM权重即可提升组织病理描述准确性，并在多个设置中取得+3.3到+19.6个百分点的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）能从组织学图像生成病理描述，但gigapixel级别的全切片图像（WSIs）超出其视觉上下文限制。标准的瓦片切分方法虽然使WSI可处理，却割裂了定义肿瘤-间质界面的组织邻域关系，导致空间上下文信息的丢失。

Method: SLMP将WSI区域表示为空间文本图：以MLLM生成的描述初始化瓦片节点，以空间邻接关系编码边。每个瓦片的LLM通过在共享聚合策略下整合相邻瓦片传来的语言消息来精炼其描述，该策略在瓦片网格上充当一种作用于文本的自适应局部核，可通过文本梯度从模型观察到的组织表型中自动优化。

Result: 在HER2和CAMELYON16代表性区域上，SLMP在通用型和病理专用型骨干模型上均提升了瓦片级肿瘤描述准确率，增益为+3.3至+19.6个百分点。随机邻居消融实验确认增益源于空间上下文而非额外文本，优化的策略揭示了可解释的组织特异性决策规则。无需任何权重更新，SLMP显著提升了通用MLLM性能并缩小了与病理专用模型的差距。

Conclusion: SLMP提供了一种透明且灵活的机制，将空间推理融入基于MLLM的病理分析中。它在不微调骨干模型权重的前提下，通过可检查的文本聚合策略实现空间推理，从局部细胞上下文到更广泛的组织形态学实现自动语义优化，为病理分析中的空间推理提供了一种人类可读、可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can generate pathological descriptions from histological images, but gigapixel Whole Slide Images (WSIs) exceed their visual context limits. The standard tiling workaround makes WSIs tractable yet severs the tissue neighborhoods that define tumor-stroma interfaces and morphology. We introduce Spatial Language Message Passing (SLMP), a framework that performs spatial reasoning entirely in language space, human-readable by construction. SLMP represents a WSI region as a spatial text graph: tiles are nodes initialized with MLLM descriptions, and edges encode spatial adjacency. For each tile, an LLM refines its description by integrating language messages from adjacent tiles under a shared aggregation policy that, on the tile grid, acts as an adaptive local kernel operating on text rather than learned embeddings. This policy is an inspectable prompt that can be refined from model-observed tissue phenotypes via textual gradients, enabling automatic semantic optimization from local cellular context to broader tissue morphology without fine-tuning MLLM weights. On representative HER2 and CAMELYON16 regions, SLMP improves tile-level tumor description accuracy in settings spanning general-purpose and pathology-specialized backbones, with gains of +3.3 to +19.6 percentage points. Random-neighbor ablations confirm that these gains stem from spatial context rather than additional text alone, and inspecting the optimized policies reveals interpretable, tissue-specific decision rules. Besides, without any weight updates or fine-tuning the backbone MLLM, SLMP substantially improves general-purpose MLLMs and narrows its gap to pathology-specialized counterparts, offering a transparent and flexible mechanism for incorporating spatial reasoning into MLLM-based pathology analysis.

</details>


### [55] [Intelligent Detection of Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) Metrics Based on 2D Floor Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14317)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实现高效楼层设计，并确定每层所需的灯具数量和类型，从而估算平面图的电力需求，减少人工设计时间并提升建筑设计与施工行业的工作流程效率。

Method: 以Mask RCNN为基线模型，对平面图图像进行标注并转换为COCO数据格式用于模型训练，实现对照明符号的检测、灯具类型识别及相关文本的提取。

Result: 模型在边界框检测上达到0.7596的mAP，在分割任务上达到0.7111的mAP；在IoU阈值为50时bbox_mAP达到0.9850，在IoU阈值为75时segm_mAP达到0.9219，表现优异。

Conclusion: 该模型能够帮助建筑和施工等行业自动检测MEP对象，缩短设计时间并创建高效工作流程，是构建能源高效建筑设计工具的第一步。

Abstract: This research developed a neural network-based model to extract various information from 2D floor plans. We detect lighting symbols, identify the appropriate type of light, and extract the associated texts with lights. The study aims to enable efficient floor designing and determining the number and type of lights needed per floor, i.e., allow efficient design and estimate the power requirement of the floor plan. The model was developed using Mask RCNN as the base. The images were annotated and converted into a Coco data format for training the model. The model achieved bbox\_mAP and segm\_mAP values of 0.7596 and 0.7111, respectively. It also performed well at different IoU thresholds, i.e., with bbox\_mAP 50 and segm\_mAP 75 values of 0.9850 and 0.9219, respectively. The developed model will help various industries, such as architecture and construction, to improve design time and create efficient workflows by automatically detecting Mechanical, Electrical, and Plumbing (MEP) objects from floor plans, and it is the first step towards building tools that will help energy-efficient building design.

</details>


### [56] [TRIAGE: Risk-Controlled Pseudo-Label Admission for Annotation-Efficient Semi-Supervised Retinal OCT Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14321)
*Md Ashraful Hossen Akash,Shyla Afroge,Abdullah Al Mamun,Md. Kishor Morol,Tze Hui Liew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TRIAGE，一个基于风险控制的半监督框架，用于OCT扫描分类。它结合了分层分类器、患者级共形风险控制器和上下文感知Transformer教师模块，在仅使用20%标记数据的情况下达到89.66%的扫描级准确率和0.9641的宏AUC，显著优于现有的六种最先进半监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT成像在视网膜疾病诊断中缺乏自动化，原因在于专家标注成本高昂。现有半监督学习的伪标签生成方法仅基于预测置信度，未考虑不同错误类型之间的不对称性（如疾病分级不足的严重性），需要一种能控制风险且考虑各种错误成本的半监督分类框架。

Method: TRIAGE框架包含三个核心模块：(1) 分层分类器，支持疾病亚型的部分异常监督；(2) 患者分组共形风险控制器，采用原始-对偶覆盖控制来管理不对称成本矩阵下的风险；(3) 上下文感知Transformer教师网络，用于跨切片验证。整体以患者级共形风险控制为核心，实现风险可控的半监督OCT分类。

Result: 在Noor眼科医院数据集上（16,822张B-scan，161名患者，554个volume），仅用20%标记数据即达到89.66%准确率、0.8805宏F1、0.9641宏AUC和8.34%的分级不足率；用5%标记数据仍保持76.88%准确率和0.1656分级不足率。与其他六种SOTA半监督方法相比显著更优，消融实验证明各模块均有贡献，分级不足率相比固定阈值方法降低42.7%。在OCT-C8数据集上，1%标记数据达到98.00%的3类分类准确率，10%标记数据达到95.94%的8类分类准确率。

Conclusion: TRIAGE通过引入患者级共形风险控制和不对称成本矩阵，有效解决了OCT分类中标注稀缺和错误不对称的问题，在极低标记数据比例下仍能保持高准确性和低分级不足率，为OCT自动化诊断提供了一种风险可控的可靠方案。

Abstract: The advanced retinal disease diagnosing imaging modality, optical coherence tomography (OCT), encounters a lack of automation because of the high expenses for annotations performed by specialists. The use of SSL solves the problem of insufficient annotations using unlabeled B-scans; however, most of the current techniques for generating pseudo-labels are based on prediction confidence without considering the asymmetry between different types of errors. This paper proposes TRIAGE, a risk-controlled semi-supervised framework for OCT scans classification, which uses the concept of a patient-level conformal risk controller with an asymmetric cost matrix. TRIAGE unites three crucial modules: a hierarchical classifier that is capable of working with partially abnormal supervision of the disease subtypes, a patient-grouped conformal risk controller with primal-dual coverage control, and a context-aware Transformer teacher for cross-slice verification. On the dataset from Noor Eye Hospital (16,822 B-scans, 161 patients, and 554 volumes) with a test set of unseen patients, TRIAGE demonstrates 89.66% scan-level accuracy, 0.8805 macro-F1, 0.9641 macro-AUC, and an 8.34% under-grading rate when using only 20% of the labeled data. With only 5% of the labeled data, TRIAGE keeps 76.88% accuracy and a 0.1656 under-grading rate. Compared with the other six state-of-the-art semi-supervised methods, TRIAGE significantly outperforms them with ablation study demonstrating the contribution of each module in the overall framework performance (by 42.7% in terms of under-grading rate comparing to fixed threshold methods). TRIAGE demonstrates 98.00% accuracy for 3-class classification with 1% labeled data and 95.94% accuracy for 8-class classification with 10% labeled data on the OCT-C8 dataset.

</details>


### [57] [Weakly Supervised Polar Low Segmentation in Sentinel-1 SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14366)
*Andrea Federici,Jakob Grahn,Giacomo Boracchi,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CREST，一种基于图像级标签的弱监督语义分割框架，用于在无像素级标注的SAR图像中分割极地低压。CREST在对抗擦除（AER）基础上引入约束有序区域扩展（CORE）模块和动态引导（DB）损失，分别利用空间连通性先验约束区域扩展、以挖掘顺序作为标签可靠性代理来减弱噪声监督，在Sentinel-1 SAR数据及两个对照组数据集上均优于标准AER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极地低压是快速形成的高纬度强烈海洋气旋，深度学习可检测但像素级分割仍具挑战。难点在于：(1) 无像素级掩码可用于训练；(2) 极地低压的边界本质模糊且主观，专家标注也不一致。因此需要仅依赖图像级标签的弱监督分割方法。

Method: CREST基于对抗擦除（AER）框架，包含两个核心创新：(i) 约束有序区域扩展（CORE）模块，编码极地低压的空间连通性先验，从高置信度种子开始约束区域扩展，避免收集无关背景特征；(ii) 动态引导（DB）损失，将挖掘顺序作为标签可靠性代理，对后期挖掘的噪声更大区域减弱监督权重。

Result: 在Sentinel-1 SAR数据上，CREST比标准AER更紧密地跟随气旋结构，并输出多类别掩码（而非二值掩码），类别表示各区域的可靠性等级。在BUS-UCLM乳腺超声和PASCAL VOC人体数据集上，相同设置下CREST均优于等效的AER流水线。

Conclusion: CREST通过空间连通性约束和动态监督权重分配，有效解决了弱监督分割中背景噪声累积的问题，在极地低压SAR分割及其它满足连通性先验的任务上均取得改进，为无像素级标注的遥感分割任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Polar lows are intense maritime cyclones that form rapidly at high latitudes. Deep learning can detect them in Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) imagery, but pixel-level segmentation remains an open challenge. No pixel-level masks are available for training, and a polar low's extent is inherently subjective, with diffuse boundaries that even experts delineate inconsistently. We propose Constrained Region Erasing with Soft Targets (CREST), a Weakly Supervised Semantic Segmentation (WSSS) framework that generates masks solely from image-level labels. Our approach builds on Adversarial Erasing (AER), which iteratively mines discriminative regions, erases them, and retrains a classifier to reveal complementary cues that become pseudo-labels for segmentation. However, standard AER also collects irrelevant background features, degrading pseudo-label quality. CREST addresses this with (i) a Constrained Ordinal Region Expansion (CORE) module that encodes the spatial-connectedness prior of polar lows, constraining region expansion from a high-confidence seed, and (ii) a Dynamic Bootstrapping (DB) loss that treats the mining order as a proxy for label reliability, attenuating supervision from noisier, later-mined regions. On Sentinel-1 SAR data, CREST follows the cyclone structure more closely than standard AER, and returns a multi-class rather than binary mask whose classes indicate the reliability assigned to each region. We further evaluate on BUS-UCLM breast ultrasound and PASCAL VOC person data, whose targets satisfy the same connectedness prior but come with the dense masks the SAR data lacks. On both datasets, CREST performs better than the equivalent AER pipeline under identical settings.

</details>


### [58] [GBU-Palm: A Multimodal Video Dataset and Benchmark for Palm Presentation Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14389)
*Yingjie Ma,Zitong Yu,Wei Jia,Ajay Kumar,Linlin Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GBU-Palm，一个大规模多模态视频掌纹呈现攻击检测（PAD）数据集和基准，包含21,326段视频、105名受试者、210只手掌，覆盖6种采集环境以及真实、打印和重放三种呈现类型，并提供6,310个同步RGB-NIR样本。通过泄漏控制协议和跨环境评估设置，揭示了环境偏移下不同视频架构的性能退化模式，以及RGB-NIR融合并非始终优于RGB单独输入的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有掌纹呈现攻击检测数据集受限于静态图像、受限采集条件或缺乏多模态视频数据，无法系统评估不同环境、模态和攻击类型下的PAD方法性能，亟需一个统一且具有挑战性的跨环境基准。

Method: 构建包含6种环境、3种呈现类型（真实/打印/重放）的大规模RGB-NIR多模态视频数据集；设计分离手掌身份和攻击血统的泄漏控制协议；在环境匹配和预留环境设置下基准测试四种代表性视频架构；通过TA/TR/FA/FR分解、频谱掩蔽、时间顺序干预和冻结骨干NIR探测等方法分析模型行为。

Result: 实验发现：环境偏移下不同架构的性能退化程度差异显著；RGB-NIR融合并不始终优于单一RGB输入；通过多维度行为分析揭示了不同架构在失败模式和证据利用方面的明显差异。

Conclusion: GBU-Palm为跨环境条件下鲁棒多模态掌纹PAD方法的开发和评估提供了统一且具挑战性的基准，并揭示了现有方法在环境泛化方面的关键局限。

Abstract: Existing palm presentation attack detection (PAD) datasets are often limited by static imagery, restricted acquisition conditions, or insufficient multimodal video data, hindering systematic evaluation across environments, modalities, and attack types. We present GBU-Palm, a large-scale multimodal video dataset and benchmark containing 21,326 videos from 105 subjects and 210 palms across six acquisition environments, including bona fide, Print, and Replay presentations, with 6,310 synchronized RGB-NIR samples. We construct leakage-controlled protocols that separate palm identity and attack lineage and benchmark four representative video architectures under environment-matched and held-out-environment settings. Results reveal substantial architecture-dependent degradation under environmental shift and show that RGB-NIR fusion does not consistently outperform RGB-only input. We further analyze model behavior through true accept (TA), true reject (TR), false accept (FA), and false reject (FR) decomposition, spectral masking, temporal-order intervention, and frozen-backbone NIR probing, revealing distinct failure patterns and evidence utilization across architectures. GBU-Palm provides a unified and challenging benchmark for developing and evaluating robust multimodal palm PAD methods under cross-environment conditions.

</details>


### [59] [Can We Defend Against AI-Generated Video Attacks on Real-World Crisis Events? A Systematic Evaluation of Detectors, Generators and Social Dissemination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14391)
*Shuo Liang,Yixing Ma,Pengfei Zhou,Xingyan Chen,Zihan Mei,Manting Li,Feihan Chen,Zhiwen Wang,Bin Xu,Haotian Zhang,Jiajun Song,Shiya Su,Run Liu,Zhenghang Ni,Yifa Yu,Jintao Hong,Bolong Feng,Yifei Liu,Zirui Zhang,Jingxuan Zhang,Songlin Zhao,Yifan Bai,Kang Tan,Yizhe Liu,Junhao Du,Yongtao Ge,Zhaopan Xv,Xinyuan Zhang,Mengru Ma,Chunhua Shen,Wei Wang,Yang You,Zheng Zhu,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-Bench基准，用于AI生成视频检测。该基准包含17,886个视频，涵盖10个社会风险类别和9种视频生成器。研究发现：现有三类检测器（传统检测器、零样本多模态模型、微调的MLLM）均无法在RA-Bench上一致泛化；生成质量、条件信息和采样种子对检测器影响各异；能够欺骗人类检测者的视频同样难以被检测器识别，且社交媒体传播进一步增加了检测难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成器可以制造逼真的战争、灾难、公共紧急事件等真实世界危机场景，带来严重的虚假信息风险。然而，现有基准缺乏对检测器和生成器在真实危机场景下行为的系统评估，包括可检测性如何随生成条件变化、人类如何感知生成视频、以及检测器在社交媒体传播过程中是否仍然可靠。

Method: 构建RA-Bench基准，包含1,830个真实视频锚点（覆盖10个社会风险类别）和16,056个由四个开源及五个闭源生成器生成的剪辑。评估沿三个维度展开：1) 评估七种传统检测器、十种零样本多模态模型（三种审查设置）和两种微调的MLLM的泛化能力；2) 分析可检测性随生成质量、条件信息和采样种子的变化；3) 研究人类真实性判断与社交媒体传播下的检测器可靠性。

Result: 三类检测器框架均无法在RA-Bench上一致泛化。生成属性（质量、条件信息、种子）对不同检测器家族影响不同，而源级检测模式在不同种子间保持稳定。能够误导人类的视频对当前检测器同样困难，且社交媒体传播使检测更难。

Conclusion: 当前方法难以检测逼真的AI生成视频，突显了开发对不断演化的视频生成器具有鲁棒性的检测器的迫切需求。

Abstract: Recent video generators can fabricate realistic depictions of wars, disasters, public emergencies, and other real-world crises, creating substantial risks of misinformation. Existing benchmarks, however, provide limited evidence on detector and generator behavior in such settings, including how detectability varies with generation conditions, how people perceive generated videos, and whether detectors remain reliable during social dissemination. To address this gap, we introduce RA-Bench, a benchmark for AI-generated video detection that uses Real videos as Anchors. RA-Bench contains 17,886 videos, comprising 1,830 real-video anchors across 10 social-risk categories and 16,056 generated clips from four open-source and five closed-source generators. Based on RA-Bench, we organize our evaluation along three dimensions. We first assess detector generalization across seven traditional detectors, ten zero-shot multimodal models under three review settings, and two MLLMs specifically fine-tuned on AI-generated video detection. Across these methods, none of the three detector families generalizes consistently across RA-Bench instances. We then examine how detectability varies with generation quality, conditioning information, and sampling seeds. These analyses show that generation properties affect detector families differently, while source-level detection patterns remain stable across seeds. Finally, we study human authenticity judgments and detector reliability during social dissemination. We find that videos that mislead people are also difficult for current detectors, and that social dissemination makes detection harder. Together, these findings show that current methods struggle to detect realistic AI-generated videos, highlighting the need for detectors robust to evolving video generators.

</details>


### [60] [IRGNN: Efficient Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for Radar Point Cloud Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14394)
*Xiao Guo,Wanke Xia,Lili Yang,Caicong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IRGNN，一种基于不变雷达图神经网络的目标检测方法。该方法将雷达点云重建为具有平移和旋转不变性的图表示，利用改进的消息传递神经网络（含残差连接和虚拟节点层）增强局部特征传播和全局上下文建模，最终通过任务特定头实现目标分类和边界框预测。在RadarScenes数据集上，IRGNN优于现有雷达目标检测方法，同时显著降低了推理时的计算成本和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LiDAR目标检测在恶劣天气下可靠性下降，而雷达点云虽对恶劣天气和低光照环境具有鲁棒性，但其稀疏、无序且信息量少的特点使现有LiDAR感知方法难以直接应用，因此需要专门针对雷达点云特性的检测方法。

Method: IRGNN首先将雷达点云重构为图表示，采用平移和旋转不变的特征设计以鲁棒地建模稀疏雷达测量；然后使用改进的消息传递神经网络（MPNN），融合残差连接和虚拟节点层来增强局部特征传播和全局上下文建模；最后通过任务特定头进行目标分类和边界框预测。

Result: 在RadarScenes数据集上的实验表明，IRGNN优于现有的基于雷达的目标检测方法，取得了具有竞争力的性能，同时显著减少了推理时的计算成本和内存使用。

Conclusion: IRGNN通过图神经网络和不变特征设计有效解决了雷达点云稀疏无序带来的挑战，在性能上超越现有方法的同时大幅提升了推理效率，展示了其在自动驾驶中高效雷达感知的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Perception is a fundamental component of autonomous driving systems. While LiDAR-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in object detection, their reliability can degrade under adverse weather conditions. Radar point clouds provide a robust alternative due to their resilience to bad weather and low-illumination scenarios. However, radar point clouds are typically sparse, unordered, and less informative than LiDAR data, making it challenging to directly apply existing LiDAR-based perception methods. To address these challenges, we propose IRGNN, an Invariant Radar Graph Neural Network for radar point cloud object detection. IRGNN first reconstructs radar point clouds into graph representations using translation- and rotation-invariant feature designs, enabling robust modeling of sparse radar measurements. It then employs an improved message passing neural network (MPNN) with residual connections and a virtual node layer to enhance local feature propagation and global context modeling. Finally, task-specific heads are applied to the learned graph representations for object classification and bounding box prediction. Experimental results on the RadarScenes dataset show that IRGNN outperforms existing radar-based object detection methods and achieves competitive performance. In addition, IRGNN significantly reduces computational cost and memory usage during inference, demonstrating its effectiveness and practical potential for efficient radar-based perception in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [61] [CRAFT: Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning for Subject Personalization without Composed Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14403)
*Jihun Park,Kyoungmin Lee,Jongmin Gim,Hyeonseo Jo,Jaeyeul Kim,Han Zou,Zhenpeng Zhan,Yan Zhang,Sunghoon Im*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT（Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning），一个无需composed-target监督的注意力级奖励微调框架，仅用1万张参考图像和主体掩码，通过LoRA微调预训练reference-aware多模态扩散Transformer（MMDiT），在FLUX.2-klein-9B上取得SOTA性能，并可迁移到其他reference-aware骨干网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主体驱动的图像个性化方法依赖大规模（数十万到数百万）配对的（参考图，合成目标图）数据，其数据构建需要昂贵且复杂的多阶段流水线（LLM提示生成、T2I目标合成、参考主体提取、VLM质量过滤和对应标注），且与特定的目标合成器紧密耦合，成本高且缺乏通用性。

Method: CRAFT基于单步ReFL框架，通过LoRA适配器微调预训练的reference-aware MMDiT，仅需1万张参考图像和主体掩码（无composed-target监督）。核心是"Where to look"原则：注意力级奖励将噪声和短语token的注意力与正确的参考主体对齐；生成的逐主体注意力掩码进一步门控像素级身份奖励，确保图像空间监督与学习的注意力路由保持一致。

Result: 在FLUX.2-klein-9B上，CRAFT在XVerseBench基准上取得SOTA性能，且仅用1万张参考样本（无composed-target监督），而先前通用方法需要15万到200万+对composed-target数据。该配方可迁移至其他reference-aware骨干网络，且稳定提升性能。

Conclusion: CRAFT证明大规模composed-target监督并非主体驱动个性化所必需，通过注意力级奖励和参考仅式数据构建即可实现SOTA性能，大幅降低数据构建成本，并提升方法的通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Subject-driven image personalization---generating new images that preserve the identity of one or several reference subjects in novel scenes---is a foundational capability for modern visual content creation. It is currently dominated by generalized methods that fine-tune a pretrained multimodal diffusion transformer (MMDiT) on hundreds of thousands to millions of paired \emph{(reference, composed-target)} examples, where each composed target is a synthesized image of the subject in a novel scene. Producing such targets demands a costly multi-stage curation pipeline---LLM-based prompt generation, T2I-based composed-target synthesis, reference-subject extraction, VLM-based quality filtering, and correspondence labeling---and tightly couples each method to a particular target synthesizer and curation choice. We introduce \emph{CRAFT} (Constrained Reward via Attention Fine-Tuning), a single-step ReFL framework that fine-tunes a pre-trained \emph{reference-aware} MMDiT via LoRA adapters using a compact reference-only data construction---$10$K reference images and subject masks, with no composed-target supervision. CRAFT realizes a \emph{Where to look} principle: attention-level rewards align noise- and phrase-token attention with the correct reference subject, and the resulting per-subject attention masks gate a pixel-level identity reward to keep image-space supervision consistent with the learned attention routing. Applied to FLUX.2-klein-9B, CRAFT achieves state-of-the-art performance on XVerseBench \rev{while using no composed-target supervision---only $10$K reference-only samples, whereas prior generalized methods require $150$K to over $2$M composed-target pairs}. The same recipe transfers to other reference-aware backbones, consistently improving performance. Project page: https://jihun999.github.io/projects/CRAFT/.

</details>


### [62] [GhostPoint: Self-Supervised Representation Learning by Hallucinating Occluded LiDAR Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14428)
*Mohamed Abdelsamad,Bin Yang,Michael Ulrich,Miao Zhang,Yakov Miron,Alexandru Paul Condurache,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostPoint，一种针对3D目标检测的自监督学习框架，通过在实例体素膨胀生成的邻域中幻觉化潜在特征，显式建模可见表面之外的遮挡和未观测区域，在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督学习方法的目标函数仅定义在激光雷达可见表面的测量返回值上，存在可见表面偏差，这对逐点任务（如分割）足够，但3D目标检测需要应对缺失结构的鲁棒性，因此需要超越可见表面建模未观测区域的方法。

Method: GhostPoint框架包含两个核心组件：1) 通过新颖的实例体素膨胀生成局部邻域，幻觉化邻域中的潜在特征；2) 编码器处理观测返回，额外的预测器从观测上下文推断邻域表示。在监督方案上，除标准的编码器级监督外，引入预测器级监督：可见/掩码体素匹配教师编码器目标，未观测体素匹配教师预测器的幻觉输出。

Result: 在nuScenes和Waymo数据集上的大量实验表明，GhostPoint达到了SOTA性能，在稀疏扫描和有限标签条件下，持续提升下游3D目标检测性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模可见LiDAR返回之外的结构信息，GhostPoint有效弥补了自监督学习在3D目标检测任务上的迁移差距，证明了预测器级监督和实例级体素膨胀在自监督预训练中的有效性。

Abstract: 3D object detection from LiDAR point clouds is a core problem in autonomous driving. Recent advances in self-supervised learning (SSL) enable scalable pretraining and transfers well to per-point tasks such as semantic and panoptic segmentation, but transfer to 3D detection remains weaker. We analyze recent SSL methods and find that most objectives are defined only on measured LiDAR returns from visible surfaces, leaving occluded and unobserved regions unconstrained. This visible-surface bias can be sufficient for point-wise prediction, but 3D detection requires robustness to missing structure. To address this gap, we propose GhostPoint, an SSL framework that hallucinates latent features in local neighborhoods around discovered instances, generated via a novel instance voxel dilation. In GhostPoint, an encoder processes observed returns, and an additional predictor infers neighborhood representations from observed context. In addition to standard encoder-level supervision, we introduce a predictor-level supervision scheme on sampled voxels from generated neighborhoods. Specifically, observed (visible/masked) voxels match teacher-encoder targets, while unobserved voxels match teacher-predictor hallucinations. This design encourages the learned representation to explicitly model structure beyond observed returns. Extensive evaluations on nuScenes and Waymo demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, consistently improving downstream 3D detection, especially under sparse scans and limited labels.

</details>


### [63] [Style or Signature? Artist-Disjoint Evaluation of Style Classification in Frozen Vision Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14435)
*Rory Ashton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑了使用CLIP等冻结图像嵌入进行艺术风格分类的高准确率，指出传统随机划分评估中同一艺术家的作品同时出现在训练和测试集，导致分类器可能通过识别艺术家而非真正理解风格来取得高准确率。作者提出艺术家不相交（artist-disjoint）评估协议，在320幅画作、四个二十世纪艺术流派的平衡数据集上进行5-NN风格分类，准确率从0.87降至0.77，且下降极不均匀：印象派和立体主义几乎不受影响，而超现实主义下降20个百分点。该模式在四种图像编码器上保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有艺术风格分类评估使用随机划分，同一艺术家的作品会同时出现在训练与测试两侧，导致高准确率可能源于对艺术家个体的识别而非对风格本身的理解，因此需要更严格的评估协议来检验模型是否真正理解艺术风格。

Method: 提出艺术家不相交协议，逐一留出每个艺术家的全部作品，确保任何作品都不会使用其创作者的其它作品进行分类。在包含320幅画作、涵盖印象派、立体主义、超现实主义等四个二十世纪流派的平衡数据集上，使用5-NN分类器，并测试四种不同的图像编码器（包括视觉-only自监督模型）以区分视觉结构与语言影响。

Result: 在艺术家不相交协议下，5-NN风格分类准确率从0.87降至0.77，下降极不均匀：印象派和立体主义几乎不变，超现实主义下降20个百分点。该模式在四种编码器中一致，包括视觉-only自监督模型，表明效应源于视觉结构而非语言信息。能捕捉真实共享形态的编码器中，个体艺术家难以识别但风格分类稳健；超现实主义则表现相反。

Conclusion: 艺术家不相交评估协议是衡量冻结嵌入中风格理解的必要条件。现有高准确率可能高估了嵌入对风格的理解能力，其中部分流派风格被个体艺术家特征所混杂，需要更严格的评估方法来可靠检验艺术风格理解能力。

Abstract: Frozen image embeddings from models such as CLIP are increasingly used to classify paintings by art-historical style, with high reported accuracy. We ask whether this accuracy reflects an understanding of style or the recognition of individual artists. Standard evaluation uses random splits in which works by the same artist appear on both sides, so a classifier can succeed by recognising the painter rather than the movement. We re-evaluate style classification under an artist-disjoint protocol, holding out every artist in turn so that no work is ever classified using other works by its own painter. On a balanced dataset of 320 paintings across four twentieth-century movements, 5-NN style accuracy falls from 0.87 to 0.77 under this protocol, and the drop is sharply uneven. Impressionism and Cubism barely move, while Surrealism falls twenty points. The pattern holds across four image encoders, including a vision-only self-supervised model, which places the effect in visual structure rather than language. Where an encoder captures genuine shared form, individual artists are barely recognisable yet style is robust, while Surrealism shows the opposite. We argue that artist-disjoint evaluation is necessary to measure stylistic understanding in frozen embeddings.

</details>


### [64] [Marionette: Predicting World States, Rendering Geometry, Painting Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14530)
*Zian Meng,Zhen Li,Chuanhao Li,Qiang Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Marionette，一种用于交互式游戏的世界模型。它显式建模可解释的3D世界状态（276维，包含骨架、轨迹和旋转），通过零参数渲染器进行精确几何计算，再由视频扩散模型合成RGB图像，从而在长时程交互中保持状态一致性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交互式游戏世界模型直接在像素或潜空间自回归生成视觉观测，迫使姿态、几何和遮挡等结构化属性被隐式维护，导致长时程预测中误差累积，一致性和可控性脆弱。

Method: Marionette包含三个组成部分：1）两阶段自回归动力学模型预测276维显式3D世界状态（多实体关节骨架、度量根轨迹和旋转）；2）零参数图形桥接层将预测状态转化为姿态控制视频，以闭式方式计算几何和遮挡；3）控制条件视频扩散观测模型合成逼真RGB观测。

Result: 实验表明：1）预测的World状态可直接控制，强制不匹配的动作流使关节误差改变31%；2）无约束时两角色漂移到21.2米远（记录会话保持在5米附近），三分之一的帧出现地面穿透；施加地形碰撞器和分离上限两项规则后，穿透减少66%且角色保持交互，无需修改观测模型；3）外观生成保真度无损，FVD为831，对比记录的831的pose。

Conclusion: 显式建模世界状态并将几何计算交给固定零参数渲染器，可以显著提升交互式游戏世界模型的长时程一致性和可控性，同时不牺牲外观生成质量。状态空间中的规则修复比在观测空间中修改更简单有效。

Abstract: Interactive game world models typically autoregress visual observations directly in pixel or latent space, forcing structured properties such as pose, geometry, and occlusion to be implicitly maintained by the same generative sequence. Over long horizons, errors in these latent world properties accumulate, making consistency and controllability fragile. We explicitly model the evolving world state, delegate exact geometric computation to a fixed, zero-parameter renderer, and leave the neural model to synthesize appearance. We instantiate this idea as Marionette, a world model for interactive games with articulated characters. First, a two-stage autoregressive dynamics model predicts an explicit and interpretable 276-dimensional 3D world state comprising multi-entity articulated skeletons, metric root trajectories, and rotations. Second, a zero-parameter graphics bridge converts the predicted state into pose-control videos, computing world-space geometry and occlusion in closed form. Third, a control-conditioned video-diffusion observation model synthesizes photorealistic RGB observations from the resulting structured controls. Our experiments establish two properties of Marionette. First, the predicted world state is directly controllable. Forcing a mismatched action stream changes root-aligned joint error by 31% across 48 held-out segments. Second, long-horizon behaviour is determined in the state, and can be repaired there. Left free, the two generated characters drift to 21.2 m apart (recorded sessions stay near 5 m) and a third of frames show ground penetration. Two rules imposed on the explicit state, a terrain collider and a separation cap, cut penetration by 66% and keep the pair engaged, with no change to the observation model. Routing appearance through the predicted state costs no fidelity we can detect, at an FVD of 831 against 799 for recorded pose.

</details>


### [65] [Decoding the Past: An Uncertainty-Aware Deep Learning Framework for Sex Attribution in Prehistoric Hand Stencils](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14539)
*Karel Becerra,Boris Mederos,Dean Snow,Ramón A. Mollineda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不确定性感知的深度学习框架，用于史前手部模绘艺术的性别归因。该框架整合了双图像处理、轮廓提取、结构化剪影增强、模型架构多样性和集成决策聚合，结合当代手部样本训练，并通过三角化验证（集成预测、潜在空间映射和可解释AI）确保解剖一致性，最终为史前性别鉴定提供预测和内部一致性置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧石器时代手部模绘（hand stencils）的性别鉴定极具挑战性，主要源于缺乏真实标签、当代与史前人群之间的种群差异以及图像退化带来的不确定性。传统形态计量方法存在性别间结构重叠大、跨种群泛化能力差和特征工程主观性强等问题，亟需一种能够显式建模和传播不确定性的稳健方法。

Method: 构建不确定性感知的深度学习流水线：(1) 双图像处理与双轮廓提取；(2) 生成每个模绘的12种剪影实现以捕捉边界不确定性；(3) 两个集成模型（各含10个EfficientNet-B3和MobileViT-S网络），基于14,036个当代手部样本训练；(4) 三角化验证方案，结合集成预测、UMAP+k-NN的2D潜在空间流形映射和LayerCAM可解释AI空间归因，确保解剖学一致性。

Result: 在当代数据上，集成模型分类准确率在较年长年龄组中超过88%。应用于史前模绘时，框架能同时输出性别预测和内部一致性置信度，有效区分形态稳定与模糊案例。集成预测、潜在空间结构和可解释性分析三者收敛，验证了不确定性作为可测量指标在考古推断中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，不确定性可以成为考古推断中可量化的组成部分，使古代岩石艺术的性别解码更加稳健和可重复，为史前手部模绘的性别鉴定提供了超越传统形态计量方法的新范式。

Abstract: Determining the biological sex of the individuals who created Upper Paleolithic hand stencils remains a challenging problem due to the absence of ground truth, population differences between contemporary and prehistoric groups, and the uncertainty introduced by image degradation. Traditional morphometric methods suffer from high structural overlap across sexes, poor cross-population generalizability, and subjective feature engineering. This study presents an uncertainty-aware deep learning framework for sex attribution in prehistoric hand stencils that explicitly models, propagates, and aggregates uncertainty throughout the analytical pipeline. The methodology combines dual image processing, dual contour extraction, structured silhouette augmentation, model architectural diversity, and ensemble-based decision aggregation. The pipeline generates twelve plausible silhouette realizations per stencil to capture boundary uncertainties, which are processed by two ensembles of ten deep neural networks each (EfficientNet-B3 and MobileViT-S) trained on 14,036 contemporary hand samples. Furthermore, a triangulated validation scheme integrates ensemble predictions with unsupervised 2D latent-space manifold mapping (UMAP + k-NN) and explainable AI spatial attributions (LayerCAM) to ensure anatomical consistency. On contemporary data, ensemble models achieve strong classification performance, with accuracies exceeding 88% in older age groups. When applied to prehistoric stencils, the framework produces both sex predictions and confidence measures of internal agreement, enabling the distinction between morphologically stable and ambiguous cases. Convergence across ensemble predictions, latent-space structure, and interpretability analyses shows that uncertainty can become a measurable component of archaeological inference, enabling robust and reproducible decoding of ancient rock art.

</details>


### [66] [MagnifiQ: Patch-aware Text Guided Progressive Upscaling for High-Resolution Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14543)
*Mahesh Reddy,Yashesh Savani,Antoine Mercier,Hong Cai,Fatih Porikli,Guillaume Berger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MagnifiQ是一个图像恢复框架，通过渐进式放大策略（如从1024×1024到4096×4096）利用预训练文生图扩散模型SDXL，将自注意力层替换为计算成本线性增长的卷积操作，并使用补丁特定文本提示词增强局部细节，在4K分辨率下实现更清晰、更连贯的图像恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像恢复需要同时保持全局结构一致性和恢复细粒度局部细节，但4K分辨率下直接基于扩散模型的恢复计算成本过高，且容易产生重复或不一致的纹理。

Method: 1) 将SDXL的自注意力层替换为卷积操作，使计算成本随分辨率线性增长；2) 提出渐进式放大策略，通过多个分辨率阶段迭代恢复并细化中间输出；3) 使用补丁特定文本提示词提供空间定位的语义引导来控制内容漂移并增强局部细节。

Result: 在合成和真实退化图像上的大量实验表明，MagnifiQ在感知质量和人类偏好上优于先前的基于扩散的恢复方法，能产生更清晰的纹理和更连贯的4K结果，并通过可扩展骨干网络和渐进式设计提供实用的速度-质量权衡。

Conclusion: MagnifiQ通过可扩展的扩散模型架构与渐进式放大策略相结合，有效解决了4K高分辨率图像恢复中的计算瓶颈和全局一致性难题，为大规模高分辨率图像修复提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: High-resolution image restoration from degraded inputs is challenging because it must preserve global structural consistency while recovering fine-grained local details, especially at 4K resolution where direct diffusion-based restoration is computationally expensive and prone to repeated or inconsistent textures. In this work, we introduce MagnifiQ, an image restoration framework that progressively upscales and restores images across resolutions, e.g., from 1024x1024 to 4096x4096. Our approach leverages a pre-trained text-to-image diffusion model such as SDXL and adapts it for more scalable high-resolution inference by replacing its original self-attention layers with convolutional operations whose computational cost grows linearly with image resolution. We further propose a progressive upscaling strategy that iteratively restores images over multiple resolution stages, refining each intermediate output rather than directly hallucinating the final 4K image, thereby improving global coherence and reducing high-resolution artifacts. To enhance local details while controlling content drift, MagnifiQ uses patch-specific text prompts that provide spatially localized semantic guidance during restoration. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world degraded images show that MagnifiQ outperforms prior diffusion-based restoration methods in perceptual quality and human preference, producing sharper textures and more coherent 4K results while offering practical speed--quality trade-offs through its scalable backbone and progressive design.

</details>


### [67] [CPI-Bench: A Comprehensive,Practical and Intelligent Benchmark for Real-World Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14546)
*Qinye Zhou,Jun Zheng,Yongchao Du,Yuan Wang,Zhengrui Chen,Zuan Gao,Taihang Hu,Chao Lin,Yefeng Shen,Xingjian Wang,Zhao Wang,Zhengtao Wu,Xiaoli Xu,Zhengze Xu,Hao Yan,Denghui Yang,Yuhang Yu,Huayu Zhang,Mingzhou Zhang,Mengting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPI-Bench，一个全面、实用且智能的图像编辑基准，包含三个子集（通用、实用、智能），覆盖多图像编辑、真实用户场景和高推理需求编辑任务，并与Arena Image Edit排行榜高度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑基准局限于简单单图像任务，覆盖维度有限，无法有效区分不同模型性能，也难以评估多图像编辑、高推理需求和实际部署场景下的模型表现。

Method: 构建CPI-Bench基准，包含三个核心子集：CPI-General-Bench（全面覆盖各类编辑任务并率先引入多图像编辑评估）、CPI-Practical-Bench（聚焦高频真实用户应用场景）、CPI-Intelligent-Bench（评估高推理需求编辑能力）。

Result: 在CPI-Bench上评估主流图像编辑模型，结果显示该基准增强了模型间的性能区分度，并通过排名分析证明了其与Arena Image Edit Leaderboard的最高对齐度，能够忠实反映人类评估者的偏好与感知判断。

Conclusion: CPI-Bench能够全面可靠地量化模型在通用编辑能力、实际部署效率和高级推理编辑方面的差距，为图像编辑模型的未来优化提供有价值的指导，并可作为真实用户体验的有效代理。
